> 3. Before a trade, count attackers and defenders; a queen is never recaptured by a lesser piece.
> 4. Ruy Lopez Morphy (a6, Nf6, Be7, O-O, d6) held well; keep it.
> 5. Watch for bishop-queen batteries on the long diagonal (h7) and forks by knights (Nb4, Nxe3+).
> 6. Count material after every capture; I lost a pawn early on dxe5 Nxe5 - recheck recaptures.
> 7. When ahead, trade queens and simplify; avoid opening my king.
> 8. Win material with undefended pieces; don't leave a knight hanging to a king (Nf5 check).
> Game 3 of 4: you play White against A02. Standard chess rules (castling, en passant, promotion; checkmate wins; stalemate, threefold repetition, the fifty-move rule and insufficient material are draws). A game that reaches 40 moves each is stopped and scored on material. A win counts 1 point, a draw half a point.
